Now I am very much terrified by my enemy; therefore I have come to you. O Muni! You protect those who come to seek your refuge; I have now come here to seek your shelter; so save me from this danger."

झशिकताव

निर्भयं त्रस रोजेन्द्र नात्र ते श्रवः किल ।

आगमिष्यन्ति बलिनो निश्चयं तपसो बलात् ॥ 37 ॥

The Maharṣi said: “O King! Stay here without

any fear; none of your enemies would be able to enter this hermitage by my power of tapasyā, even if they be very powerful.

नात्र हिंसा प्रकर्निया वनवृत्त्या नृपोतम ।

कर्तेयं जीवनं शस्तै-निंवारफलमुतकैः ॥ 38 ॥

O Best of Kings! You will not be allowed to kill animals here; you will have to sustain yourself on this wild rice, roots and fruits, etc., as the rules of the forest living permit.

व्यास उवाच

इति तस्य चयः शुत्वा निर्भयः स नुपस्तदा ।

उवासाभप्र एवासी फलमूलाशनः शुधि: ॥ 39 ॥

Vyāsa said: Thus hearing his words, the King began to live there, with all purity and without any fear, on roots and fruits.

कदाधित्य नुपस्तत्र शुक्ष्छ्छायां समाधितः ।

चिन्त्यामास चितातों गृह् एव गताशय: ॥ 40 ॥

राज्ये मे शुत्रधिः प्राप्तं सेन्द्रेः पायाते: सदा ।

संपीडिता: स्युतोंकास्तैतुंचारोरितोत्रिः ॥ 41 ॥

Once the King, while taking rest under the shade of a tree, while thinking of various things, thought of his own house thus: "My enemies have, no doubt, acquired my kingdom, but they are vicious and wicked, shameless Mlecchas and always addicted to sinful deeds; certainly they are tormenting my subjects.

गजाछ़ तुराग: सर्वे दुर्बलान भट्यवर्जितः ।

जाताः: स्युनीत्र सन्देशः: ग्रुशणा परीपितिहतः ॥ 4 2 ॥

My elephants and horses are not regularly getting their food and have all become powerless; certainly they are suffering very much from my enemies.

सेवका मम सर्वे ते शनुर्णां वश्चतिनतः ।

दुःखित एव जाताः स्युः पालिता ये यमा पुग ॥ 4 3 ॥

All the servants that were nourished by me before are now all suffering from troubles, having been subjected by my enemies.

धनं मे सुदुर्घारिसुद्वायपरे: परैः ।

द्वृतासवभुविष्यादिव्याने स्यामाग्रिगत किल ॥ 44 ॥

The wicked enemies are certainly squandering

Book V Chapter XXXII

away my hoarded wealth to bad immoral purposes in gambling, drinking and in revelling with prostitutes.

कोश्रार्यं कलिथन्ति व्यसनैः पायबुद्धः ।

न पाखदानपिणुणा नलैच्छास्ते मनिजणोपे में ॥ 45 ॥

Those Mleccchas and my ministers are always intent on vicious acts; they do not know who are the proper persons to be given charities; so they will no doubt exhaust away my coffers in doing sinful acts."

इति चिन्तापरो राजा वृक्षमूलस्थितो यदा ।

तद्वाजगाम वैश्यस्तु काश्रदातिपरस्तथा ॥ 46 ॥

While the King was thus meditating, seated at the root of a tree, there came one man of the Vaiśya caste looking very distressed.

यूरेण पुरतो दृष्टः पार्थे तजोपरेशितः ।

पणछ्त तं गूपः कोजीस कुण एवागातो वनम् ॥ 47 ॥

The King saw and instantly bade him take his seat beside him; then the King asked the Vaiśya: "O Noble One! Of what caste are you? Whence are you coming to this forest? What is your name? What for you look so pale and distressed? What calamity has befallen to you?

कोसि कमाव्छ दौनोसि सहितः शोकपीडितः ।

बृहि सर्वे महाभाग मैत्री साप्तपरीं मत ॥ 4 8 ॥

O Good One! Two persons become friends whenever they speak seven words amongst them; according to this rule I am your friend; tell me, therefore, truly all these things."

व्यास उवाच

तच्छुला वचनं रात्रसमुवाच विशोनम: ।

उविव्यय स्थिति भूत्वा मत्वा साधुसूसामात्म ॥ 49 ॥

Vyāsa said: The Vaiśya, hearing these words from the King, took his seat and felt himself much relieved and thinking that he has met with a saint, began to speak thus:

धैर्य उवाच

मिगाई धैर्यजातीयः समाधिनानि विश्रतः ।

धनवान्यर्मिनुण: सत्यवाननमुक: ॥ 50 ॥

"O my Friend! I belong to the Vaiśya caste; my name is Samādhi; I was rich, never I had my any jealousy towards anybody; always I used to speak truth and was devoted to religious acts.

पुत्रवृत्तरिंस्तरोई धनलुब्धीरसाधुधिः ।

'कृपणेति मियं कृत्वा त्यक्त्वा मायां सुदुस्त्यजाम् ।

स्वजनेच सर्वस्तः प्राणतोद्यिन वनागुरु वै ॥ 51 ॥

कोशिस त्वं भायवात्यामासि कथयस्य प्रियाधुना ।

My wife and sons are very greedy of money and are irreligious; so they cut off all their affections and connections with me, very difficult to cut though, and have driven me out of the house on the pretext that I am very miserly. Thus forsaken by my relatives, I have now come to this forest. You look to be a fortunate man; therefore kindly, O Dear One! give me now your introduction and oblige.

राजाधव

सुयो नाम राजाऊं हरसुधिभः पीडितोभवम् ॥ 52 ॥

प्राणोटिभ्य गतराज्योउर अमिनिधः परिवहितः ।

विद्या त्वमत्र मित्रं मे मिलितोसि विशोतम ॥ 53 ॥

The King said: “I am the King Suratha; lately I had a defeat from the dacoits; moreover my ministers deceived me; consequently I am deprived of my kingdom and have now come here. O Best of Vaśiyas! Fortunately you have come to me today as my friend.

सुखेन विहृतिथावो वनेत्र शुभपादेपे ।

गोके तव गहावृष्टे सस्थो भव विशोधनम ॥ 54 ॥

अत्रेच च यथाकामं सुखं तिह मया सह ।

We two will repose here gladly in this beautiful forest covered with trees. O Intelligent One! Now quite your sorrow; be calm and quiet and rest with me, at your leisure, here happily."

སྟོལ་ཀྱི་བྱི

कुटुर्बे मे निरालबं मया शीने सुखःखितम् ।

भविष्यिति च चिन्तातं व्याशिकोपेलपतिभन् ॥ 55 ॥

The Vaïsya said: "O King! My friends and relatives must have been helpless, very sorrowful

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

and they are distressed at my absence; they must have been troubled very much by diseases and misfortunes no doubt and have become very anxious.

भार्विद्रे सुखं नो वा पुरवेईे न वा सुखम् ।

इति चिन्तातुंरं चेते न वे शाप्यति भूयिपि ॥ 56 ॥

O King! I cannot remain quiet; my mind is being troubled with the thought how my wife and sons are spending their times now—in pain or happiness?

कटा उस्ये सुतं घायाँ गृहं स्वजनमेव च ।

स्वस्थं न मवनगो राजनूहिष्चिताकुलें भूश्म । ५७ १ ॥

I am always thinking when I would see again my sons, wife, relatives, friends, acquaintances and my house? I cannot make me calm and quiet."

राजोबाच

वैनिरस्तोत्र सि पुरावीरसद्धुर्जे: सूबालिजीः ।

तान्यूहा किं सुखें तेउघा घबिध्यति महामते ॥ 58 ॥

The King said: “O Intelligent One! What pleasure can you expect to see your wicked sons and treacherous relatives who have driven you out of your house?

हितकारी वर: श्रादुर्ःखादा: सुहदः कुतः ।

तस्माल्स्वं मनः कृत्वा विहार्त्व मया सह ॥ 59 ॥

Even the enemies are far better, provided they do good to us; what sorts of friends are they who impose on us afflictions and sorrows. Do you, therefore, make your mind calm and quiet and remain here in greatest peace and happiness."

ईपर उचाच

मनो मे न स्थरं राजन्मवत्यहा सुदुःखितम् ।

चिन्तायाज कुटुब्बस्य दुस्त्यजस्य दुरातमिधः ॥ 6० ॥

The Vaiśya said: “O King! Even those that are wicked and cruel cannot quit their relatives. Today my mind is greatly agitated with the thought of my relatives; I cannot remain quiet.”

राजोवाच

ममापि राजजं दुःखं दुनोति किल मानसम् ।

भृछाजोडा मुनि शान्ते शोकानाशमीक्षायम् ॥ 61 ॥

The King said: “My mind too, is incessantly troubled with the thought of my kingdom. Come; let both of us go to the Muni and ask him what is the medicine for the cure of these our mental agonies.”

व्यास उवाच

इति कृत्वा मार्ति तौ तु राजा वैय्यक्षु जमतुः ।

भूमि तौ विनयोपेते प्रधुं शोकस्य काराणम् ॥ 62 ॥

Vyāsa said: “O King! Thus making their determinations, they went humbly to the Muni to ask him what were the causes of their sorrows?

गत्वा ते प्रपिष्यताह् राजा ऋषिमनुत्तमम् ।

आर्याने सव्यगराने: शान्ते शान्तिम्युपगताः ॥ 63 ॥

इति श्रीमद्वीभागवते महापुराणे पद्यकन्वभे

द्वार्षिणो5यायः 11 32 11

The King then went close to him and bowing down before him, took his seat and began to ask calmly and quietly the Muni who was sitting calm and serene.

Here ends the Thirty-second Chapter of the Fifth Book on the King Suratha's going

to the forest in the Mahāpurānam,

Śrimaddevibhāgavatam of 18,000 verses

by Maharṣī Veda Vyāsa.

[1] 2017年1月1日

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

CHAPTER XXXIII

On the Greatness of the Devi

राजोवाच

मुने वैश्योउयम्युना बने मे शिरतां रातः ।

पूर्वद्वर्िरिंस्तोई प्राप्तोउर्र मम संभामम् ॥ १ ॥

The king Suratha said: "O Muni! This Vaiśya

is now become a friend of mine in this forest; he has been expelled from his home by his sons and wife and he has come lately here in this forest.

"कुटुब्बविरहेशणासी दुःविह्नोतीव दुर्भानः ।

Book V Chapter XXXIII

न शान्तिमुपायायेप तथैव मम साम्यतम् ।

गतराजस्य दुःखेन शोकातोंस्मि महामाते ॥'

निकाणर्ण च मे चिंता इद्यात्र निवर्तेत ।

हया मे दुबैलतः स्युः किं नजा: श्रवुरर्ग नातः ॥ २ ॥

He is now suffering very much from the bereavement of his family and has become very much troubled in his mind. He is not getting any peace whatsoever. I am also become like him and have become very distressed owing to my kingdom being robbed away. This thought, though really devoid of any substantial cause, is not leaving my heart now. Oh! My elephants and horses, now under my enemies, have become weak.

भृत्वरर्भेल्हा दुःखी जात: स्यतुग मवा विजा ।

कोशुर्धयं कार्यिन्ति चिपतोडिलताखणात् ॥ ३ ॥

इत्येवं चिन्तयानस्य च मे निद्रा तनी सुखम् ।

जानामीदं जगन्मिष्या स्वप्नवस्तमेवेच हि ॥ 4 ॥

My servants are suffering very much owing to my absence! My enemies will, within no time, squander away forcibly all my hoarded riches. This thought is not giving me any happiness; nay, I cannot get any sleep owing to this care and anxiety.

जान्तोरीय मनो घान्तं न स्वियं भवति प्रभो ।

कोई के3घा गना: के3मी न ते मे च सहोदारः ॥ 5 ॥

न पुत्रा न च मित्राणि येषां दुःखं दुनेति माम् ।

प्रभोउयिति जानाभि तथापि मम मानसः ॥ 6 ॥

O Lord! I know that this world is false as a dream; yet my mind is so deluded that I cannot make me quiet. Who am I? What are those horses and elephants to me? They are not my brothers, sons, nor friends; yet I feel very much for them and am troubled with their troubles. O Muni! I know these all are delusions; still I am not able to make mymind free from them.

मोहो नैवापसति किं तत्काणमढुतम् ।

स्वामित्यमसि सर्वतः सर्वसंशयनागक्त् ॥ 7 ॥

कारणं बृहि मोहस्य मासस्य च स्वामित्ये ।

This is very wonderful indeed! What is the

cause of all this? O Lord! Nothing is veiled from your sight, you are fully able to solve all these doubts. Therefore, O Ocean of mercy! Kindly explain to me and this Viśya the cause of all this delusion."

व्यास उवाच

इति पृष्ठस्तदा राजा सुमेधा मुनिसस्तमः ॥ 8 ॥

तमुवाच परं जानं शोकमोहित्यानगम् ।

Vyāsa said: “O King! When the King Suratha asked thus, the Muni in reply said to him the following words, full of wisdom, so that his delusion and sorrow might vanish.”

झरिग्वताव

भृगु राजन्यवश्यामि कारणं बन्धमोक्षयोः ॥ 9 ॥

महामाथीत विद्यालाया सर्वेयं प्राणिनामिक ।

The Muni said: “O King! I am telling you the cause of bondage as well the cause of bondage as well the cause of release of all the beings in this Universe. She is known as Mahā Māyā. She is the Mūlā Prakṛti, the state of equilibrium of the three Gunas, Sattva, Rajas and Tāmas.

बह्वा विष्णुसंशासन्सुराषड्वकर्णो5नितः ॥ १० ॥

सर्वे देवा मनुवाया नन्ववौरगाराक्षसाः ।

वृक्षाक्ष विविधा बल्ल्वः पशवो मृगपाक्षिणः ॥ १ १ ॥

मायाधीनाक्ष ते सर्वे भाजर्न बंधेमोक्षणेः ।

Even Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Maheśvara, Indra, Varuna, Vāyu, and the other Devas, Gandharvas, Nāgas, Rāksasas, men, deer, animals, birds, trees and various kinds of creepers all are under Māyā; thus they are all bound; again they all get release when they are released by that Māyā.

तया सृष्टिमितं सर्वे जनस्थावरजंभम् ॥ १ २ ॥

तद्धगे वर्तेत नूनं मोहजारेतन यंत्रितम् ।

By Her is created all this world, moving and not moving, all the beings are caught in Her net and all are under the control of Her.

त्वं किंवानानुभेदेकः क्षत्रियो वसानउर्जितः ॥ 13 ॥

ज्ञानिनामपि चेतांमि मोहयत्वत्निरं हि स ।

You are a Kṣattriya; so Rajoguna preponderates

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

in you and your heart is thus rendered impure. She, by Her Māyā, deludes even the minds of those who are Jūnāns or wise; you are but an ordinary man compared to them.

क्रेशशासुरेवाठा झाने सत्यपि शेपतः ॥ 14 ॥

तेषि रागवशाल्लोके क्षमति परिणोहिताः ।

Even Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Maheśa, though possessed of vast wisdom, still roam, under the sway of Māyā, in the three worlds completely deluded by their attachments to the sensual objects.

पुरा सत्ययुगे रात्निथनुर्गरायण: स्वयम् ॥ 15 ॥

शेत्राधीयं समासादा चकार विवृतं तपः ।

O King! In the Satya Yuga, in ancient times Visnu Nārāyaṇa himself performed a very hard tapasyā in Śvetadvīpa.

वर्षाणामयुतं यावद् इद्यविद्याप्रसक्तये ॥ 16 ॥

अनश्यसुखायासी चिन्तयानस्ततः परम् ।

He passed away full ten thousand years in meditation, with the object of attaining the unbroken everlasting Bliss and becoming steadfastly attached to Brāhma Vidyā.

एकसित्धिजनै देये बह्ञाउपि परमाङुते ॥ 17 ॥

स्थिततपसि राजेन्द्र मोहस्य विनिवृतये ।

O King! Brahmā, too, became engaged in performing a tapasyā, meditating the Primordial Force, Ādyā Śakti, in a very wonderful solitary place for the cessation of delusion.

क्वाधिष्ठापुरोदेजसी खलोतमातिरहितः ॥ 18 ॥

तस्माहोत्समुदाय वगमान्यदिह्वशा ।

चतुर्मुंवोर पीजेन्द्र तदैव नि:सूतः खलात् ॥ 19 ॥

मितिली मार्गमध्ये तु चतुर्मुंखचतुर्ष्जी ।

Once on a time Vāsudeva Hari wanted to go to another place; he got up and started to see other places. Brahmā, also, left his place and started for another destination.

अन्योयं गुर्वन्ती ती करलं करलविमिति स्म च || 20 ||

बहा प्रोवारा ते देवं कर्ताई ज्ञान: किल ।

When they met each other in their way, each one asked the other "Who are you?" The Prajāpati answered: "I am the Creator Brahmā."

विष्णुस्तमाह घो मूर्खं जगत्कर्तांहमच्युतः ॥ 21 ॥

त्वं किंव्यान्वहीनोरिस्त्र नतोरुणमवमाश्रितः ।

Hearing thus the Brahmā's words, Viṣṇu said: "O You Stupid! I am Acyuta Viṣṇu; therefore I am the Creator of this world." You are inferior to Me as there is so much of Rajoguna in preponderance in you.

सत्वाशितं हिं मां विविट्र वासुदेवं सन्तानम् ॥ 22 ॥

माया तं रक्षितोदीव कृत्वा युद्धे सुदारुभणम् ।

शरणं मे समायातो दानवाभ्यां प्रपीडितः ॥ 23 ॥

मया तो निर्हती कामं दानवी मधुकैट्यपी ।

Know Me as the eternal Vāsudeva, preponderating in Satīva Guna. Do you not remember that I fought a dreadful battle for you and thus saved your life a short while ago. I slew the two Dānavas Madhu and Kaitabha when you were much distressed by them and took My refuge.

कर्व गर्वायसे मंद मोहोरयं त्वंस सामप्रम्भ ।। 24 ॥

न मतोघ्याधिकः कश्रिसंसारोभिन्यमातिरे ।

How then do you boast now! O Fool! Quit your this vain boasting now. In this wide world, there is none superior to Me.

ब्रह्मविद्याच

एवं प्रवद्यानी ती ब्रह्माविष्यु परस्परम् ॥ 25 ॥

सुकुरदोषी वेयमानी लोहिताओं बभूवतुः ।

The Rşi said: “Thus engaged in disputing with each other, their lips were quivering with anger and their eyes got red.

प्रावुर्बपूव सहसा तयोर्विद्वदमानयो: ॥ 26 ॥

मध्ये लिंग सुधाश्रं तिवपुतं दीर्भीमद्धुतम् ।

When, Behold! there appeared suddenly between two disputants, a nectar-like white phallic emblem (Lüngam), wonderfully long and extensive.

आकाशे तरसा तत्र वागुवाचागरीस्थि ॥ 27 ॥

तो संबोध्य महाभागी विवदन्ते परस्पत् ।

Then a voice, from without anybody, broke out in the Heavens and addressed Brahmā and Viṣṇu who were quarrelling thus?

ब्रहाविष्णो विवादं मा कुतःतां वां परस्पत् ॥ 28 ॥

लिंगस्यास्य परं पारमधस्तादुपरि ध्रुवम् ।

Book V Chapter XXXIII

यो याति युवयोमीय से श्रेष्ठी वां सदैव हि ॥ 29 ॥

एकः प्रमातु पातालयाकाशमपरो5दृना ।

Whoever amongst you will be able to go to the other end of this Liṅgam whether beyond its top or below its bottom, he is certainly the superior of you two; let one of you therefore go down to Pātāla and let the other go up to you Heavens.

प्रमाणे मे चव: कार्यं त्वक्या वादि निर्विक्यम् ।। 3० ॥

मध्यस्प: सर्वदा कार्यों विवादोउस्मिन्नयोरिष्ट ।

Leave off your useless disputations and take my word as proof. It is always advisable to select an umpire to decide such a quarrel as this that has sprung up between you two.

ऋषिकवाच

तच्छुत्वा वचनं दिव्यं सजीभूतों कृतोघमौ ॥ 3 1 ॥

जममतुमितुमप्रस्थ लिङ्गम्बुतदार्जनम् ।

The Rsi said: O King! Hearing thus the divine word, both of them became ready and began energetically to measure the length of the wonderful Liṅgam that stood in front of them.

पातालमामदिष्णुबंद्रासुघाकागसे व ३३ २ ॥

परिमानुं मृतालिकुं स्वमहत्वविद्धुद्रे ।

Viṣṇu went down to Pātāla and Brahmā went up to Ākāśa to measure the Liṅgam and thus to ascertain their superiority.

विष्णुरीना क्रियहेशं श्रान्तः सर्वान्तया वतः ॥ 3 3 ॥

न प्राप्तनं स रिंगमाय रपितृवरा यथी स्थलम् ।

Going down some distance Viṣṇu got tired and doing his best, when he not find out the end of the Liṅgam, he returned and remained at the desired meeting place.

ब्रह्मासणछत्तश्छीर्ध पत्तिते केतकीद्वलम् ॥ 34 ॥

शिवस्य मस्तकताप्य परावृतो मुदापृतः ।

On the other hand, Brahmā was ascending to the skies when he got one Ketakī flower dropping from the head of the Lingam. He became over glad and returned also to the desired meeting place.

आगत्य तसरा जहा विणाये केतकीदलम् ॥ 35 ॥

दर्शपीथला च वितथमुवाच मदमोहितः ।

Brahmā became very much elated with vanity and when he returned, he at once showed that flower to Viṣṇu and spoke thus the false words:

लिङ्गस्य मस्तकाटेतदगुहितं केतकीदात्म् ॥ 36 ॥

अभिज्ञानाय चानीतं तत चित्तप्रणानाये ।

“O Viṣṇu! This Ketakī flower has been obtained from the head of the Lingam. I have brought this to you simply that you would recognise it and be convinced in your heart.

शूला तद्बहागणो वाक्यं बृद्धा च केतकीद्वम् ॥ 37 ॥

हरितं प्रत्युवाचेदं साही कः कथायपुना ।

यथार्थवादी मेघावी सदाचारः शुचि: समः ॥ 38 ॥

साही भवति सर्वैर विवादे सपुचिष्यते ।

Hearing these words of Brahmā, Viṣṇu saw the Ketakī flower and said: "O Brahmā! Who is your witness in this matter? He whose words are true, who is equal to all, who is intelligent, pure, and always of good conduct, he can be the witness in such matters of dispute.

ब्रहीावाच

दूरदेशात्समायाति साकी कः समये5रुना ॥ 39 ॥

यत्सर्व तद्वच: सेयं केतकी कथयिध्यति ।

इत्युक्त्वा प्रोत्त तत ब्रहणा केतकी सुकृष्म ॥ 40 ॥

वचनं प्रात तरसा शार्डिंणं प्रत्यवोध्यत् ।

शिवमूर्ति स्थितां ब्रह्मा गृहीत्वा यां समाताः ॥ 41 ॥

सद्रोहोउन के कत्वीस्थत्वया विजणो कदाचन ।

Brahmā said: “Who will come now as witness from that far off place? This Ketakī flower is the witness; this will give evidence.” Thus saying, Brahmā requested Ketakī to give evidence; Ketakī soon replied thus to convince Viṣṇu. “O Viṣṇu! I was on the head of Mahādeva; Brahmā has brought me from there down to this place; you ought not therefore to have any doubt on this point.

मग वाक्यं प्रमाणं हि बह्ञा पारबृतोउरह च ॥ 42 ॥

गृहीत्वा मां समयात: शिवभक्तैः समर्पिताम् ।

My word is the evidence; Brahmā has gone to the other end of the Liṅgam. Some devotee of Śiva put me on His head and Brahmā has got me down from there."

Srimaddevibhāgavatam

केतक्या वचनं श्रुला हरिराह स्मविश्व ।। 43 ॥

महादेव: प्रमाणं मे यद्यासी वचनं वदेत् ।

Hearing thus the words of Ketakī, Viṣṇu was very much astonished and said this: “I cannot trust your word; if Mahā Deva comes and speaks this himself, then I can trust and take it as a proof.”

ऋषिक्वाच

तदाकपर्यं हरेवाक्यं महादेव सनातनः ॥ 44 ॥

कृपितः केतकीं प्राह मिथ्यावादिनि मा वद ।

The Rṣi said: O King! The eternal Mahā Deva, hearing the words of Viṣṇu, spoke thus to Ketakī with great anger “O Liar! Do not utter such false words : You dropped down from My head and Brahmā while ascending up, picked you up on the way.

गच्छतो मध्यतः प्राणा पतिता मस्तकान्मय ॥ 4 5 ॥

भिथ्याधिपिणीय तयता मयां द सही ।

बह्ना लज्ञापरो भूत्वा ननाम मधुसूदनम् ॥ 4 6 ॥

शिशेन केतकी त्यथा तद्विताकुसुमेषु वै ।

Now as you have told a lie, I will never take you; you are henceforth forsaken by Me." Brahmā was then very much put to shame; he bowed down to Viṣṇu; Mahā Deva, forsook the Ketakī flower from that date.

एवं मायाकलं विद्ये जानिनामपि मोहदूम ॥ 47 ॥

अन्येधी प्राणिनां राजन्का बाता विषभं प्रति ।

O King! Such is the power of Māyā; when need to speak of other ordinary mortals!

दैवानां कार्यसिद्धार्थ सर्वदैव सापानिः ॥ 48 ॥

दैत्यान्वड्यवते घासु त्यक्त्वा पापघर्व हरि: ।

See! Viṣṇu, the Lord of Lakṣmī, is self-deluded and is always deceiving the Daityas for the welfare of the Devas, without any fear whatsoever of the sin that he is thereby incurring.

अवतात्करो देवो नानायोभिपु माध्वः ॥ 49 ॥

त्यक्वासउनन्द्युर्दं दैत्यैयुंद्धे वैवाकारोदिषुः ।

Though He is the Lord of all yet He has to take several incarnations in several wombs, forsaking the pleasures of the Heavens and fighting with the

Daityas. O King! Viṣṇu is omniscient and He is the Lord of this world; specially

नूनं मायाखलं चैतन्याध्वेप्रि जगदुगृति ॥ 50 ॥

सर्वेत्रे देकताकार्थि का वातों-न्यस्य भृपते ।

He is the only One, Supreme in the creation of the Gods. Now when Māyā exercises such a powerful influence on Viṣṇu, what wonder is there that the other ordinary beings would be deluded by Her?

ज्ञानिनामयि चेतांसि परमा प्रकृति: किल ॥ 51 ॥

बलादाकृष्य मोहाय प्रपछति महीपने ।

O King! That Highest Prakṛti draws away violently the hearts of the wise and drags them down into the ocean of world.

यया व्यानिद्र सर्वे भगवल्या चराबगम् ॥ 52 ॥

मोहाद्र जान्तद सेव बन्यमोक्षप्रदा सदा ।

That Omnipresent Bhagavatī is ever the cause of bondage of all when She casts Her net of delusion and She is again ever the cause of liberation when She imparts Her knowledge to them.

राजोवाच

भगवबृहि मे तस्य: खरूषं बलमुतमम् ॥ 53 ॥

उत्पत्तिकाणं चापि स्थानं परमकं च वत् ।

The King said: “O Brahma! What is the nature of Her? and what is the Supreme Force? What is the Cause of this creation? And where is Her highest place? Kindly narrate all these to me.

ऋरिष्टवाच

न चोत्पतिरनादित्वापुव तस्याः कदाचन ॥ 54 ॥

नित्यैव सा पा दैवी कारणानां च कारणम् ।

The Rşi said: “O King! She is beginningless; therefore She had no origin at any time; that Highest Devi is Eternal and She is always the Cause of all Causes. (How then can any other be powerful like Her).

वर्तते सर्वभूतेषु शक्ति: सर्वात्मना नृप ॥ ५५ ॥

गववच्छक्तिहीनस्तु प्राणी भवति सर्वथा ।

O King! She resides in all the beings as the

Book V Chapter XXXIII

essential vital Force; deprived of that Force, every being is reduced a dead carcass.

विष्छक्ति: सर्वभूतेषु रूपे तत्यासदेव है ॥ 5 6 ॥

आविष्यवित्तोगचौते देवानां कार्यसिद्धये ।

She is pervading as the Universal Force of Consciousness in all the beings. The form of this Śakti (Force) is the form made up of consciousness itself, the Brahma. (For the force of Fire is Fire itself; it is not seen in any other form).

यदा सुवर्तीत तां देवा मनुजाश्व विशेषाने ॥ 57 ॥

प्रभुभविति भृतानां दुःखानागय चाधिक्या ।

Her appearances and disappearances at times are simply for serving the purposes of the Gods. O King! Whenever the Devas and men worship Her, Ambikā makes Her appearance visible to destroy their pains and sufferings.

नानाङपधरा देही नानाशक्तिस्मतिता ॥ 58 ॥

आतिबर्षिति कार्यार्थ स्थेच्छया परमेक्षी ।

She assumes various forms and possesses various powers. That Highest Iśvarī comes down of Her free will to serve Her some purpose or other. देवाधीना न सा देही यथा सर्वे सुरा नृर ॥ 59 ॥

न कालक्षणा नित्यं पुरुषाध्वरातिनी ।

अकतां पुरुषो रहा दूर्घं सर्वरिंदर जगत् ॥ 60 ॥

She is not like the Devas, under the control of Daiva or Fate; She is not under the influence of Time (as both Fate and Time are created by Her). She puts always every being to action according to his capacity. Puruṣa is not the Doer; He is simply the Witness.

दूर्यस्य जननी सेव देवी सदसद्वातिका ।

पुरुषं रंजयलेका कृत्वा बढ़ाण्डनाटकम् ॥ 61 ॥

This whole Universe is the object seen. That Devi is the Mother of all this that is witnessed. She is the Manifested and She is the Unmanifested and She is the Effect also. She alone is the Actress and manifests thus the world and thus gives the colouring to the Puruṣa.

रॉजते पुरुषे सर्वे संहरल्पितरंहसा ।

तया निमितभपृताते ब्रदायिषणामहेक्षरा: ॥ 62 ॥

कल्पिता: सव्यकाव्येयु प्रेगता तीलात्य त्वयी ।

When the Puruṣa is coloured thus, She destroys quickly these worlds. It is said that Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Maheśa are respectively the Creator, Preserver and Destroyer of the world; but this is merely a statement; really they are merely instruments in Her hands.

स्वांगे तेषु समारोष्य कृतातसे बल्वतरा: ॥ 63 ॥

दत्ताश्र शनकसदेभ्यो गीर्वेर्मीर्गिरिता सत्या ।

Bhagavati has created them in reality for Her Pastime and stationed them in their respective posts. She has bestowed to them Her part manifestations, i.e., Sarasvati to Brahmā, Lakṣmī to Viṣṇu, and Girijā to Maheśa and has thus rendered them more powerful.

ते तो ध्यापनि देयोगा: पुजपत्नि पर्ग मुदा ॥ 64 ॥

जात्या सर्वेश्रीं गर्जिके सृष्टिश्वितिनागरीनाय ।

एतते सर्वमाद्यातं देर्वीमाहात्ययुतमम् ।

मम बुद्धमनुसारेण नानं जानाभि भुपते ॥ 65 ॥

इति श्रीमद्वीधीगानते महापुराणे पछामकल्पे

यर्षिवर्शो3यायः ॥ 133 ॥

They, the lords of the Devas, always meditate and worship Her as Creatrix, Preservrix and Destructrix of this Universe. O King! I have thus described to you, as far as my intelligence and knowledge go, the holy greatness and the excellent glory of Her (in reality, I have not been able to come to the end of it).

“Aim, Hrim, Klim Cāmundāyai bicche” is the (9) nine lettered mantra.

Here ends the Thirty-third Clapter of the Fifth Book on the description of the greatness of the Devi in Śrīmaddevībhāgavatam, the Mahā Purāpam, of 18,000 verses by Maharṣi Veda Vyāsa.

CHAPTER XXXIV

On the Worship of the Devi

राजोबाच

भाववन्दूरि मे सत्यकन्या आरापने विधिम् ।

पूजारिविं च मन्योक्ष तथा होमविंधि वद ॥ १ ॥

The King said: O Bhagavan! Kindly narrate to me in detail now the methods how to serve and worship the Goddess and the Mantrams that are used on such occasions.

ऋरिकवाच

शृणु राजन्यवक्ष्यामि तस्याः पूजाविशुं शुभम् ।

कामवं मोक्षवं नृणां ज्ञानदं दुःखनारुम् ॥ 2 ॥

The Rsi said: “O King! I am now describing the method how to worship the Goddess. Hear. This leads to the fulfilment of all desires, to the liberation from one’s bondage, to self-realisation and to the destruction of all miseries.

आदी सनापिधिं कृत्वा गुधि: शुकलापवो नरः ।

आचय प्रवतः कृत्वा शुभमायतनं निजम् ॥ ३ ॥

The worshipper has to perform his bath; then putting on a white cloth, he will have to perform his Vaidika and Tāntrika Sandhyā; then he should, with his heart controlled, perform his Ācamana a ceremony and select a good auspicious site for his own Pūjā purposes.

ततोउलिपतभूम्यां तु संस्थायासनुभनम् ।

तत्रोपधिप्य विधिप्रवित्रचच्य मूर्यान्वतः ॥ 4 ॥

पूजाद्यैं सुसंस्थाय यथाशक्यनुसारतः ।

प्राणायमं ततः कृत्वा भूतगृद्धि विधाय च ॥ 5 ॥

कुर्णवीर्णाप्रतिशं तु संघांर प्रोक्ष्य मंत्रतः ।

काल्नानं ततः कृत्वा न्यासं कुर्णीधाविधि ॥ 6 ॥

शुभे ताममये पात्रे चन्देन सितेन च ।

यद्कोर्ण विलिखिडोत्रं चाहकोणं ततो कहि: ॥ 7 ॥

नवाक्षरस्य मनस्य बीजानि विलिखेत्ततः ।

कृत्वा चन्द्रगितईं च वेदेत्नां संविधाय च ॥ 8 ॥

अर्शा वा धातर्वी कुर्मोलुजामनैः शिक्षोदितैः ।

पूजनं पृथिवीपाल भगवल्या: प्रयन्ततः ॥ 9 ॥

कृत्वा वा विक्रियलुजामागमोक्तां समाहितः ।

जयेत्रबाक्षरं मन्त्रं सत्ततं ध्यानपूर्वकम् ॥ 10 ॥

होमं द्रशणशतः कुर्महिंशोजेन च तर्पणम् ।

भोजनं ब्राह्मणानां च तद्धशोजन कारयेत् ॥ 11 ॥

चरित्रयपाटं च नियं कुर्मीढ़िसज्वैत् ।

नवरावतं चैव विधेयं विनिपूर्वकम् ॥ 12 ॥

Next he should plaster the site with cow-dung and spread his sacred carpet (Āsana) whereon he is to take his seat with a cheerful mind and sip water for Āśamana three times. Then he is to collect the articles for worship according to his best capacity and place them duly in their respective position. He is to perform Prānāyāma (regulated his breath); and then follows the Bhūta Śuddhi, the purification of the old and the formation of the celestial body and Jīva-Śuddhi by which the Sādhaka becomes the Devatāmaya; he then proceeds to Mātrkā Nyāsa (i.e., setting mentally in their several places in the six Cakras and then externally by physical action the letters of the alphabet which form the different parts of the body of the Devatā. He then places his hand on different parts of his body, uttering distinctly at the same time the appropriate Mātrkā for that part).

Bhūta-Śuddhi: Dissolve earth into water, water into fire, fire into air, air into ether into Aharhākra, Aharhākra into Mahat and Mahat into Prakṛti, the final Cause. This process is called Bhūta-Śuddhi.

He is to mention then the time, date, tithi, and month of the year and make his Sankalapa; then he will have to assign to the different parts of his body the Mātṛkā Mantras duly as well as his own Mantram; next he is to meditate in his own body the seat of the different Devatās and do the internal worship. He is to breathe life into the Deity outside to be worshipped as well as within himself to be meditated and worshipped; then he is to do the same with the articals for worship and purify them

Book V Chapter XXXIV

by sprinkling with water and Astra or Phat Mantram, thus removing all sources of obstacles that are likely to interfere with the act. Next, on an auspicious copper plate, he is to draw inside a six-angled (hexagonal) figure (two triangles crossing each other with their vertices one upward and the other below) with white sandal paste or with eight perfumed things and outside this figure, an octagonal figure of eight petals; outside this he is to draw the boundary lines that is called the Bhūpura. On each of the eight petals he is to write each letter of the nine-lettered Bīja (Seed) Mantram and the ninth letter in the central ovum. Next by the Mantram by which breath is infused or by the Vedic Mantram he will have to place the Yantra in the proper position and then worship the Ādhāra Śakti (the Vital Force) in the central ovum and the holy seat with the Pitha Mantrams. He will have to invoke the Devi, uttering the Seed Mantram over a golden plate or figure and carefully worship Her by offering seats and other articles duly as enunciated in the Yāmala Tantras etc. Then he will have to perform the six-fold worship of the Ganas in the six angles and worship Indra, etc., and Vajra and others in the Bhūpura (the boundary), and thus finish the Pūjā of the Yantra. (For the Pūjā see the Prapañca Sāra.) Note.—Bhūpura is what is thought over outside front or in the beginning. Here the Gana Devatās are first thought over and worshipped. Worship outside, worship inside and See the Deity in and out, everywhere and be free” is the motto of the worship. In the absence of the Yantra, one will have to make a metalled image of Bhagavatī and worship Her with the greatest caution with the Mantras as expounded by Śiva in the Tantrams (of Yāmalā and others). Note: Yantra is that which restrains. This human body is the Yantra. And its imitators is placed outside in various shapes and figures. The Yantra is the mystical diagram used by the dovetes for worship.—Or one may use

the Vaidika Mantrams in worshipping the Deity in accordance with the prescribed rules and with his mind controlled; then, merged in meditation, one is to mutter silently (perform the Japam of) the nine-lettered Mantram. (The Mantram is Krim, Dakṣine, Kālike, Svāhā). Japam (muttering or repeating silently the Mantram) is of two kinds—Nitya (daily) and Pauraścaranik (repetition of the name of the deity accompanied with burnt offerings). In the Nitya Japam, Nitya Homas are performed and in the occasional Pauraścaranik Japam, one tenth of this is offered; Abhiṣeka, too, is one-tenth of this Homa; Tarpanam is one-tenth of Abhiṣeka and the feeding of the Brāhmanas is one-tenth of what is done in the Tarpanam. O King! Thus completing the Japam one is to read daily the Canḍī (do the Candīpātha) where the three glorious deeds of the Devi are narrated; next he will have to allow the Deity invoked to depart to Her own place. The Navarātra Vrata (nine night vow) is next to be observed according to the proper rites and ceremonies. Hrīm Mahiṣa Mardinyai Svāha is the Mantra.

अधिंचे च तथा चैवे गुर्जले पढ़े नातिप्र १

नवरायावसायों वै कर्तेल्य: शुभपिच्छता ॥ 13 ॥

In the bright fortnight of the month of Śāvin or Caitra, is to be observed the fasting of the Navarātra by those who desire for their own welfare.

होम: शुविषुतः कावोंं जयमनंः सुगवसः ।

गर्कनैरावृतमिश्री धन्युक्तैः सुसेंकृतैः ॥ 14 ॥

Homas are to be offered, many in number, and Mantrams are to be recited, the same as in one's own Mantram, good Pāyasam with sugar, ghee, and honey mixed is to be offered in this ceremony.

डागमांसेन वा कायां बिल्वपरैतंस्वा शुधेः ।

द्वाराकुर्णी रेन्थिनलैवां शक्रकायुतः ॥ 15 ॥

Goat meat, or holy leaves of the Bel tree, or red Karavīra flowers or til (sesamum seed) mixed with honey can be used instead in the Homa ceremony.

अध्यमं च चतुर्वयं नवम्यां च विशेषतः ।

कत्वेयं पूजनं देया ब्राह्मानां च भोजनम् ॥ 16 ॥

The special days for the worship of the Devi are the eighth, ninth, or fourteenth day (tithi) of the half month. The feeding of the Brāhmins must be done on each occasion.

निर्मीयं धन्यानोति रोगी रोगात्मुष्यने ।

अपूजी लभने पुत्राङ्छणांशे यशालनिंनः ॥ 17 ॥

O King! Thus the poor become wealthy, the diseased get cured, and the persons that have no issue get obedient and well qualified sons.

राज्यश्रो गोराज्य प्राप्नोति सावर्धीमिकम् ।

श्रविधि: यौवित्ती हृति रिर्युं मयाप्रसात्तः ॥ 18 ॥

The King, expelled from his kingdom, gets back by the grace of Mahā Māyā, dominion over the whole earth and becomes able to destroy all those enemies of his, by whom he was before vanquished, when he worships the Devi.

विद्यार्थी पुर्जनं यस्तु क्रोगे तिवर्पेतिः ।

अनवहां शुभां विहां विहेंं नात्र संशयः ॥ 19 ॥

The persons, desirous of learning, get undoubtedly the learning honourable and auspicious, provided he worships the Devi with his senses restrained.

ब्राहणः क्षिवौये कैश्यः शुत्रो वा फक्तिसंयुतः ।

पूजयेज्ञानां धात्री सर्वसैयुखाभगवेदे ।। 20 ॥

Persons of all castes, Brāhmīns, Ksatriyas, Vaiśyas or Śūdras can become masters of all pleasures and happiness provided they worship with devotion to the Devi, the Preserver of the World (the Jagaddhātri).

नवरात्रजतं कुयांत्रस्नारीणाङ्ग यः ।

वांछितं फलमाणोति सर्वदा भक्तित्यतः ॥ 21 ॥

आधिंने शुक्लपक्षे तु नवरात्रजत शुभम् ।

क्रौलिं भावसंयुक्तः सर्वां-कामानवान्युताः ॥ 22 ॥

A man or woman whoever performs the Navarātra vow always full of devotion, gets all the desired fruits. Whoever celebrates the holy Navarātra ceremony in the bright fortnight of the

month of Aśvin with his heart full of the thought of the Devi, gets all his desired fruits.

विविधान्यजलं कृत्वा पूत्रास्थानं प्रकलयेत् ।

कलर्श स्थायपेतच येवदान्यविधानतः ॥ 23 ॥

O King! Now I am describing the rites and ceremonies; here a square raised platform or altar is to be made according to the prescribed rules; a water jar is then to be placed on it with the Vedic mantrams and due rites and ceremonies.

यन्ये सुर्विरं कृत्वा स्थायपेकलशोगरि ।

वापवित्वा वर्ग्रांश्रात्याध्रश्न: परिवर्तितान् ॥ 24 ॥

One will have to make a beautiful Yantra according to the previously laid rules and the water-jar is to be placed on it; then spread the beautiful Yava grains all around the jar.

कृत्वोपरि वितानं च पुष्पमातासमावृतम् ।

यूर्वपित्युर्वयुक्ते कत्विं चरिककारुम् ॥ 25 ॥

An awning or pandāla is to be erected over the altar and the place of worship, and the site is to be decorated with flowers. Lights and Dhūpas, incense and perfumes are then to be used in the hall of the Candikā Devī.

त्रिकालं तत्र कतंच्या पूजा शक्तयनुसारतः ।

विशरणाक्यं न कतवं चशिककायाङ्ग पूजने ॥ 26 ॥

O King! The Devi is to be worshipped thrice; morning, midday and evening; no miserliness is to be shown in spending wealth for this purpose.

धूर्वैर्दिंष: सुनेवेधे: फलपुष्पेनकशः ।

गतिजादीः स्तोजपार्तैर्वद्यागरणैस्या ॥ 27 ॥

उत्सवस्त कत्हीयो नानावादिर्वसर्युतः ।

Light, dhūp, good presents of rice and other edibles, flowers, and fruits of various kinds are to be offered in this worship of the Devi; the chanting of the hymns of the Vedas, songs, and music with the various instruments are to be done and a grand festivity is to be made.

कन्यकानां पूर्वनं च विधयं विधिपूर्वकम् ॥ 28 ॥

चन्द्रनेपूर्णवैलैर्भधैक्षे ध्रुवित्या ।

सुगन्धतैलागलैश मनसो कृचिकारकै: ॥ 29 ॥

Moreover, note this carefully that virgins are

Book V Chapter XXXIV

to be worshipped duly with sandal, ornaments, clothing, various edibles, sweet scented oil, and beautiful garlands. (This worship of the virgins of the virgins is one of the essentials.)

रूवं सम्पूजनं कृत्वा होंमं मन्त्रविधानतः ।

अध्यायां वा नव्यां वा कारयेढ़िपूर्वकंम् ॥ 30 ॥

Thus completing the worship of the Devi, Homa is to be done duly with Mantrams and other necessary articles on the eighth or the ninth tithi.

ब्राह्माणामोवेयश्रात्मारणं द्रगभिति ।

कर्तव्यं शक्तितो दानं देयं भक्तिरपरैपैः ॥ 31 ॥

Lastly the Brāhmins are to be fed duly; then the worshipper is to take his first meal after fasting (i.e., make pānaṃ) on the tenth day; then presents and various articles are to be offered to the Brāhmins according to one's might and with devotion.

एवं यः कुन्ते भक्ल्या नवरायजतं नरः ।

नारी वा सध्वा भक्ल्या विषया वा पतितान ॥ 32 ॥

O King! Any man, or any chaste married woman or a chaste widow whoever performs thus the Navarātra Vrata gets in this world all the desired fruits and enjoys all sorts of enjoyments and gets unbounded happiness and after death goes to the highest place.

इह लोके सुखं भोगान्यापोति मनस्पेलितान् ।

देहान्ते परमं स्थानं प्रापोति ब्रतत्पर: ॥ 33 ॥

जन्मानरोप्रिकवाफिकर्मवेल्याथिवारीणि ।

जन्मोतमकुले गाय सदाचारो भवेद्रिक सः ॥ 34 ॥

And if, owing to some cause or other, he has to take his birth again in this world, he would be born in an excellent and would become endowed with good conduct and qualifications and get the unflinching devotion towards the Ambikā Devī.

नवराजत्रत प्रोक्ते ब्रतानामुत्तमं ब्रतम् ।

आराधनं शिवायास्तु सर्वसौख्यकं परम् ॥ 35 ॥

O King! I have thus described to you the rules of the Navarātra ceremony; this vow is the best of all; highest and greatest pleasures and happinesses are obtained in worshipping thus the auspicious Mahā Māyā.

अनेन विधिना राजनस्माराधय चण्डिकम् ।

बिला पिणुसखिलतं राज्यं प्राप्यस्तानुतमम् ॥ ३६ ॥

सुखं च परयं भूप देडे.प्रिमन्यगृहे पुनः ।

पुत्रदारानसामासादा तप्यसे नात्र संशयः ॥ 37 ॥

O King! Better worship Caṇḍikā duly according to the prescribed rules; then you would be able, by Her grace, to conquer all your enemies and you will regain your excellent dominion, unshaken by any, and you will get again the highest pleasure and happiness when you will be reunited with your wife and sons in your own palace; there is no doubt in this.

वैश्योतम लवेयाड़ समाराधय कामदान् ।

देवीं विश्रेष्ठशी माघा सृष्टिसंहारकारिणी ॥ 38 ॥

O Vaiśya! You, too, better worship the same Mahā Māyā, the Goddess of the Universe, worshipping Whom leads to the fructification of all desires.

स्वजनानां च मान्यस्तं चविध्यास गृहे गतः ।

सूखं सांसारिकं प्रायय यथाभिलिविं पुनः ॥ 39 ॥

देवीलोके शुभे वासो भवित्त ते न संशयः ।

You will then be able to regain all your wordily pleasures in your own home and be respected by your relatives and acquaintances and finally, after your death, you will go to the holy abode of the Devi.

नाराधिता भगवती यैसेने नरकभागिनः ॥ 40 ॥

इह लोकेशितु:खालाँ नानारोर्षे: प्राणिडितः ।

There is no doubt in this. Those that do not worship the Devi, go to Naraka or hell; moreover they suffer much from various diseases in this world.

भवन्ति मानवा राजब्धुर्विश्र घराजिताः ॥ 41 ॥

निष्कलजा हुशुजाशु रूणानीः सत्यकुद्धयः ।

Those that do not worship the Devi are always defeated by their enemies, are void of wife and sons, become stupid and suffer pains from their unsatisfied desires.

बिल्वीदतेः कार्यैरः शनपरेष्ठ चमपकैः ॥ 42 ॥

अशिता जानतां धात्री यैसेतोरनि विलासिनः ।

And those that worship the Preservix of this

world the Bel leaves, Karavīra flowers, Śatapatra and Campaka flowers, that blessed man, devoted to the Devi, gets filled with all sorts of enjoyments.

भवन्ति कृतपुण्यासे शक्तिभक्तिपरायणा: ॥ 43 ॥

घनविभवमुखाख्या मानवा मानवतः

सकलगुणागणानां भाजनं भारतीशा: ।

निर्मापतिदात्मनः: पूजिता वैभंबानी

नृपतितिलकमुख्यासे भवन्तीह लोके ॥ 44 ॥ ॥

इति शीर्द्वैभागवतो महापुराणे पत्रमस्कन्मे

वतुसिंशोरधाव: 11 34 11

O King! What more can I say than this, that

those who have worshipped the Devi Bhavānī with the Mantrams approved by the Nigama Śāstras, those very persons get honour in this world and are filled with all sorts of power and wealth. Verily, they stand foremost in the rank of best men, becoming the only repositories of all the best qualities in this world.

Here ends the Thirty-fourth Chapter of the Fifth Book on the methods of the worship of the Devi in Śrimaddevibhāgavatam, the Mahā Purānam of 18,000 verses by Maharsī Veda Vyāsa.

[1] 2017年1月1日

Šrimaddevībhāgavatam

CHAPTER XXXV

On the King Suratha's Getting the Boons and on the Vaiśya Samādhi

व्यास उवाच

इति तस्य वय: श्रुत्वा दुःस्तिती वैश्यपार्थिवंति ।

प्रिणपत्य मुनिं प्रीत्या प्रश्रावनन्तीं प्रुगम् ॥ १ ॥

Vyāsa said: “O King! Hearing thus the Rsj’s words, the king Suratha and Vaśya, who were very distressed in their minds, became very much comforted and bowed down to the Muni with great humility and modesty.

इर्शणोतृष्तनयनावृतुवार्म्यकोविदी ।

कृताज्ञिलपुटी शान्ती बफित्यणचेतोर्षि ॥ २ ॥

Their eyes expressed their gladness and their hearts were filled with loving devotion. Both of them, then, clever in speaking and of calm and quiet temper, began to address him with their folded hands.

भगव्याचितावष्ठ शान्ती दीनी शुचाचिति ।

तब सुक्सारस्वथा गड़वेव भगीरथः ॥ ३ ॥

साध्वः सभ्यवन्तीह परोयकृतितत्पराः ।

अकृकिमगुणागामः सुखद्वः सर्वदैर्वितान् ॥ ४ ॥

O Bhagavan! We were passing our days in a very humble and distressed spot; we are today purified by your good words, just as the country was entered pure by Bhagiratha when he brought down the river Ganges here.

पूर्वेण्ययुस्त्रैन प्राणतोर्यमाशय: शुभः ।

त्वावाष्प्यं महाभाग महादुःखविनाशकः ॥ 5 ॥

The saints, adorned with purely good qualities, are incessantly engaged in doing good to others and how the people can be made happy.

भवन्ति मानावा भूमौ बह्वः स्वार्थतयतः ।

परार्थसाधन दक्षा: कैकित्वसार्थ पिवारुगाः ॥ 6 ॥

O Intelligent One! Surely we have come to this auspicious Āśrama owing to our past good deeds (in previous births) and all our miseries are therefore brought to their ends today.

दुःवितोर्ध सुनिश्रे श्रेयोर्ध चातिपु:वितः ।

उभी संसारसंतनो तनाभ्रमपदे मुदा ॥ ७ ॥

There are good many persons that roam in this world for their selfish ends; very few there exist like you who are always ready to do good to others. O Muni! True that I am very much distressed but this Vaśya is more distressed than me.

दर्शनाटवे हैं विद्वानां दुःखमिहावयोः ।

देहर्ज मानसं वाक्यमश्वणदेव साम्पत्न ॥ ८ ॥

Both of us, very much afflicted by the miseries of world, have come gladly to your Śārama and are relieved of our bodily sufferings by your sight; and now, hearing your words, we are relieved also of our mental pain and sufferings.

ध्यानावारां कृतकृत्यी जाती सूकिसुपारासत् ।

पाविती भवता बहु-कृपया करणणार १ १ १

O Brāhmaṇa! We are very much blessed and

Book V Chapter XXXV

our objects have been gained by your nectar-like words; O Thou, the Ocean of mercy! You have purified us, out of your unbounded mercy.

यूहाणास्मकरी साधो नय पारं भवाणीवात् ।

मनौ शंज्ञाविति ज्ञाला मन्त्रदोनेन संग्रदम् ॥ 10 ॥

We are quite tired of this world; knowing this, do you lead us beyond this world by holding our hands and by initiating us with Mantrams.

तपः कृत्यातिविषुले समायाध्य सुखप्रदाम् ।

संग्रण्य दर्शनं भूयो याच्यावो जिनजनित्रम् ॥ 11 ॥

O Best of Munis! We will first of all practise a very hard Tapasyā (asceticism) and worship Bhagavatī, the Awarder of happiness; then, seeing Her, we will go to our respective abodes.

वदनातल सम्राग्य देरीमन्ते नवाक्षगम् ।

स्पर्गं च करिष्यावो निर्गहारी धृततन्ते ॥ 12 ॥

Now we expect the nine-lettered Mantram of the Devi from your mouth and practising the Navarātra warm we will fast and meditate on the Mantram.

Note: The nine-lettered Mantram is “Om Mahisamardinayi Svāhā.” Instead of Om, any of the following may be used: “Hrim, Klim Aim, Strim, or Hūm mentioned in Śāradā Tilaka, Nārāyaṇī Tantra, or in Viśvasāra Tantra (see page 125 of Tantra Sāra).

व्यास उवाच

इति सञ्चोदितसाध्यां सुभेय मुनिसनमः ।

ददौ मन्त्र शुभं ताथ्यां ध्यानबीजुर्ज:सनम् ॥ 13 ॥

Vyāsa said: O King! When the king and Vaiśya prayed thus to the Muni Sumedha, the best of that Munis, gave them the auspicious Mantram with its seed (Bīja) and as well what is to be meditated (Dhyāna).

तै च प्राय युनेर्मंत्र समान्य नुरुदेवतां ।

जग्मनुर्धैश्यराजानी नदीतीरमनुत्तमम् ॥ 14 ॥

On getting the Mantram (with Rsi, Chanda, seed Sakti, and Devatā) duly, they welcomed the Muni and with his permission went to the holy bank of a river.

एकान्ते विजने स्थाने कृत्यासनपरिष्ठम् ।

उपविष्टी स्विगर्ज़ी तालतील कृणोदरी ॥ 15 ॥

Both of them were of delicate frames and both of them were fully determined; they went to a very solitary place and selected their place and took their seats there.

मन्त्रबाज्यती शान्ती चरित्रव्यपाठकते ।

निव्यनुर्मामेकं तु तत्र छायापरायणे ॥ 16 ॥

There they spent one month in repeating silently the Mantram and in chanting the three glorious deeds of Candī.

तयोर्मसतैनैव जाता ग्रीतिनुतमा ।

पदाम्बुजे भवान्यासु विश्या कृष्टिसत्वाणयतम् ॥ 17 ॥

In this short period of one month, they became very much attached to the lotus-feet of Bhavāni and their minds were also much pacified.

कदाचित्यादयोगीता मुनेस्तस्य महात्मनः ।

कृतप्रणामावगत्य तत्कृतछ्छ कुशासने ॥ 18 ॥

नान्यकार्यपरी क्वापि ध्युपतु: कदाचन ।

देवीध्यानपरि नित्यं जममन्त्रत सदा ॥ 19 ॥

They attended to no other business; only they used to go to the Muni once a day and bowing down before him they returned to their own seats of Kuśa grass and gave themselves up to the meditation of the Devi and always repeated silently their Matrams.

एवं जाते तदा पूर्णे तत्र संवत्सरे नृप ।

बध्ववृतः फलहारं त्वलत्वा पाणिज्ञो नृप ॥ 20 ॥

O King! One year thus passed away; they then abstained from taking fruits and subsisted on the leaves of trees.

वर्षमेकं तपस्तत्र चक्रतर्गैर्यपार्थिवी ।

गुखकपाणिजी दान्ती जपध्यानपाराणी ॥ 21 ॥

Thus engaged in meditation and asceticism they passed away another year sustaining themselves with dry leaves only.

पूर्णे वर्षेयुने जाते कदाचिद्धर्नंचे च तो ।

प्राप्तः स्वपमध्ये तु भगवत्या मनोहरम् ॥ 22 ॥

O King! When the two years thus passed, they got in their dreams the beautiful vision of the Goddess Bhagavatī.

रक्ताम्बराशं देर्वीं चाफभूषणभूषिताम् ।

कदाप्रिवृष्णितः स्वदेपेयपण्यज्ञान्द्विक्काम। 23 ३ ॥

वीक्ष्य स्वपे व अ ती देर्वीं प्रीतिवृत्ती बभूवतुः ।

जलाहारैस्वृत्तीये तु स्विती संवलसरे तु ती ॥ 24 ॥

They were very much delighted to see in their dreams the Ambikā Devi in red robes and decorated with various ornaments. They practised tapasyā in the third year with water as their only food.

एवं वर्षत्रयं कृत्वा ततन्ती वैश्यपार्थिवी ।

चक्रतुर्ली तदा चिन्तां चिन्ते एरंगनलासली ॥ 25 ॥

Thus when they found that, after practising the tapas for three years, they could not see face to face the Devi they became very anxious to see the Devi and thought thus.

प्रत्यक्षरूपं देवना न प्राप्त शान्तिन्दं नृणाम ।

देहत्यागं कार्यिक्यो दुःस्तिधो भुगमातुर्गे ॥ 26 ॥

"When we have not been so fortunate as to see the Devi, Who art the Bestower of peace and happiness to the human beings, we will then leave our bodies, in deep distress and sorrow!"

इति संचित्य नमसा राजा कुण्डे चकार है ।

त्रिजोर्ण सुविर्यं सौम्यं हस्तमाजप्रमाणतः ॥ 27 ॥

संस्थाय पावकं राजा तथा श्रेयो3तीभक्रिमान्

ग्राहावासी निर्ज मांसं छिल्चा छिल्चा पुनः पुनः ॥ 28 ॥

Thus thinking, the King prepared a beautiful triangular Kūndā (pit), firm and of one hand measure. Lighting a fire in that pit, the King began to cut off slices of flesh from his own body and offered them as oblations to the fire.

तथा वैश्योरीय दीपेजनी स्वामांस प्रापक्षिप्पताः ।

क्रीप्रेण वार्ति चापये द्वदुतुरी कृतोद्याए ॥ 29 ॥

The Vaiśa, too, then did the same. O King! Both of them were very much excited and began to offer their blood as oblation to the Devi.

तदा भगवती दत्ता प्रत्यक्षं दर्शनं ततोः ।

ग्रह ग्रंतििगोरद्धोती कृद्धया ती कुःवित्ती कृष्णम् ॥ 30 ॥

The Devi Bhagavatī, then, seeing then thus grieved, and that their hearts were overflowen with devotion towards Her, appeared direct before them and said thus:

श्रीद्व्यवाच

वरं वरय भो राजन् वने मनसि वांतिभान् ।

तृहा5ई तपसा तेउड घफ्नो5स त्वं मते माम ॥ 31 ॥

वैश्यं प्राह तदा देवी प्रसज्ञाई महामते ।

किं ते३भीई तद्वम्यघ्न प्राथयागु मनोपातनम् ॥ 32 ॥

“O King! You are my favourite devotees; I am pleased with your Tapasyā; now ask whatever you desire; I will grant you that boon.” Then She spoke to the Vaiśya: “O Highly Fortunate One! I am pleased; ask without any delay any boon; I will grant that just now.

व्यास उवाच

तच्छूता वचनं रजा तामुवाच मुदावितः ।

देहि मेऊढ़ निज नराचं हतशुबर्ब वन्ता ॥ 33 ॥

तमुवाच तदा देवी गच्छ राजजिंत्र गृह्म ।

शत्रवः क्रीणतच्चाले गविष्यन्ति पराजिताः ॥ 34 ॥

Vyāsa said: O King! Hearing thus the words of the Devi, the king Suratha was very much delighted and said thus: "O Devi! Grant me this boon that I be able today to conquer my enemies with my own power and that I may regain my kingdom." The Devi then spoke to him thus: "O King! Go to your own abode; your enemies are now enfeebled and will certainly be defeated.

Note: The Devi, has now withdrawn Her own power from the enemies with which they were filled before. This is the result of the real sacrifice to the Devi.

मन्थिपास्तु समागत ते पशिष्यित पादयोः ।

कुरु राज्यं मद्धापान नगरे सर्व यद्यापुकम् ॥ 35 ॥

O Fortunate One! Your ministers will all come and prostrate themselves before your feet and will be obedient to you; you can now go back to your city and govern your subjects happily.

कृत्वा राज्यं सुविशुतं वर्षाणामवुतं नृप ।

देहान्ते जन्य संप्राय्य सूर्याच्छ भविता मनुः ॥ 36 ॥

O King! Thus reign for Ayuta years (10,000 years) over your widely extended dominion; then when, you quit your body, you will again be born from Sūrya, and be known widely as Śavarni Manu.

Book V Chapter XXXV

यास उवाच

वैय्यस्तामयुवाविदं कृतांजलिपुटः गृषचि: ।

न मे गृहेण कार्यं वै न पुवेण धनेन वा ॥ 37 ॥

सर्वे बन्धकारं मातः स्वपलब्धशंरं सुकटम् ।

Vyāsa said: O King! The pure-natured Vaiśya said with folded hands: "O Devi! I have nothing to do with house, sons, nor wealth. O Mother! The house, wealth and sons, all these are so many sources of bondage to this world and are very transitory like dreams.

ज्ञान में देहि विश्वरं मोक्षदं बक्यपाराणम् ॥ 38 ॥

असारोर्स्थिश्छे संसारे मृढ़ा मज्जिति पाशाः ।

पीढ़ता: संतरीतीह तमाजेच्छलित संसृदिति ॥ 39 ॥

Therefore give me knowledge so that my ties to this world be cut asunder. Persons who are devoid of knowledge, those fools are merged in this ocean of world. The wise never prefer this Sarināra; therefore they can cross this world."

व्यास उवाच

तदाकर्णं महामाया वैश्यं प्रात पुरः स्थितम् ।

वैश्वयं तव जानं भविष्यति न संशयः ॥ 40 ॥

Vyāsa said: O King! Hearing this, the Mahāmāyā said to the Vaiśya, that stood in front of Her thus: "O Vaiśya! No doubt you will acquire knowledge."

इति दत्वा वरं ताथ्यां तरेवितार्थीयत ।

अर्जीनं नतायां तु राजा तं मुनिसतमम् ॥ 4 1 ॥

प्राणय ह्यामास्हा गतमाय मनो दर्धे ।

Thus granting boons to them, the Devi then and there disappeared. After the Devi had disappeared, the King bowed down to the Muni, mounted on his horse and expressed a desire to go back to his kingdom.

तदैव तस्य सचिवास्त्रागाल्य नृपं प्रजाः ॥ 42 ॥

प्रणेभूर्विनयोपेतास्तमृषु: प्रांजलिस्थितः ।

राजसेन शगवः सर्वे पापाच्छ निर्हता रो ॥ 43 ॥

राज्यं निष्क्रौकं कृप कृक्व पुरमाधिस्तः ।

Just at that time all his ministers and subjects came humbly before him, bowed down to him and

standing before him with folded hands, said: "O King! Your enemies all had acted very sinfully; hence they were all slain in battle; you be pleased now to remain in your city, free from any enemy and govern your subjects.

तच्छुल्चा वचनं राजा नत्वा ते मुनिसत्तमम् ॥ 44 ॥

आपच्छा निर्वेयी तत मान्धिभः परिवारिति: ।

The King, hearing thus, bowed down to the Muni and with his permission, started towards his kingdom, surrounded by his ministers.

संग्राय च निजं राज्य दारात्मवजनबोधायन् ॥ 45 ॥

बुभुजे पृथिवीं सर्वां ततः सागरमेखलाम् ।

On regaining his own kingdom, wife, relatives and kinsmen he began to enjoy the sea-girt earth.

वैश्योर्षि ज्ञानमासादा पुक्तसर्ग: समन्तः ॥ 46 ॥

कालातिवाहनं तन मुक्तबन्धधकार है ।

तीर्थेयु विचरनायभ्यमालया गुणानथ ॥ 47 ॥

On the other hand, the Vaïsya became illumined with the Spiritual Knowledge and all his connections and attachments being completely severed, became free from all bondages. He became liberated in his life-time and travelled always from one place of pilgrimage to another and passed away his time in singing the glorious deeds of the Devi.

एतते कवितं देल्याश्रितं परमाढ़तुम् ।

आराध्यनकलापारिवरीयावद्धुपवैश्ययो: ॥ 48 ॥

दैत्यानां हननं प्रोक्ते प्रतुषांविसथा शुभः ।

O King! Thus I have described to you the most wonderful character of the Devi, what fruits were obtained by the King and the Vaiśya on their worshipping Her, how the Daityas were killed by Her and about her auspicious appearances on this earth.

एवंप्रभावा सा देवी भक्तानामभयप्रदा ॥ 49 ॥

यः शृणीति नरो निव्यमेतावल्यानुत्तमम् ।

संग्रापोति नरः सत्यं संसारसुखमद्धुतम् ॥ 50 ॥

Oh! Such is the glory of the Devi, leading to fearlessness amongst Her devotees. The mortal who hears constantly this excellent pure narrative

of the Devi Bhagavatī, gets truly all the best and wonderful pleasures of this world.

ज्ञानंद मोक्षंदं चैव कीतिंदं सुखदं तथा ।

पावनं श्वणानृप्मेतदाख्यानमदूनम ।। ५। ॥

No doubt anybody who hears this wonderful incident, will obtain knowledge, liberation, fame, happiness and purity.

अर्जालायंदं नृणां सर्वोर्भमेवातून ।

धार्मिकामोक्षाणां कारणं परं मतम् ॥ ५2 ॥

The essence of all religious lies in this narration; therefore it leads, above all, to Dharma, Artha, Kāma, and Mokṣa (religion, wealth, desire and liberation). It grants all desires to human beings.

सूत उवाच

जनमेजयेन राज्ञासौ पृष्टः सत्यवतीसूतः ।

उवाच संहितां दिव्यो न्यासः सर्वांशत्चवित् ॥ 53 ॥

Sūta said: O Rṣis! The Maharṣī Vyāsa, the son of Satyavati, versed in all the departments to him this divine Sarñhitā.

चरितं चण्डिकावास्तु शुभदैत्यवधाश्रितम् ।

कथयामास भपावान्कृष्णः काकणीको मुपिनः ।

इति व: कथितः सारः पुराणानां मुनीधारा: ॥ ५४ ॥

इति श्रीमदौबीभावतं महापुराणे3शदासाहक्वां सहितायां

पत्रकन्चे पत्रविरोधध्या: 11 35 11

व्योमांकाध्विसंच्यातः 2090 श्लोकैक्यांसेन धीमता

श्रीमद्वीभागलतस्याय पडुमकन्य इग्रितः 11 5 II

समाप्तोउं घडुमः स्कन्यः

The character of Caṇḍikā, the killing of the Daitya Sumbha, were thus narrated by the merciful Muni Veda Vyāsa. O Munis! I, too, have described to you the main points of this Purāṇa.

Here ends the Thirty-fifth Chapter of the Fifth Book on the receiving of the boons by the King

Suratha and the Vaiśya Samādhi in the

Śrīmaddevibhāgavatam, the Maha Purānam,

of 18,000 verses by Maharṣi

Veda Vyāsa.
